# VTG 1:4 45 Trans Unmatched Quick Slot Report

Generated: 2026-08-17T15:15:41.369Z

This report covers every currently generated 1:4 45 Trans source pattern: both Diamond and Box shapes, all 36 cells, all four starting beats, every transition-length option, and Normal, Quad, and Second modes.

A listed source is valid and its Q2-Q5 extraction still occurs. "Does not match" means the generated Quick Slot animation could not be classified as a known VTG or QTR catalog pattern by the current matcher. Quick Slot URLs are intentionally omitted.

Cell labels use the project convention requested during development: left header first, then top header. The internal reference is included separately for code-level investigation.
